# Index reconciliation

[Baseline](BASELINE.md) · [Coverage](COVERAGE.md)

All unique linked targets in each of the eight index subsections are checked, including contextual links rather than only the first cell. Repeated target links within one index are deduplicated. These counts are link targets, not counts of distinct HTML objects. External targets are dependency references, not local definition gaps.
